# Supporting types

Generated by `bun run docs:protocol`. Edit source schemas, then regenerate.

JSON Schema validates structure. `x-ace-constraint` names additional semantic rules that clients must enforce; Zod remains the full validator.

## AccountAssignment

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountAssignment.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| instanceId | no | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| role | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":64} |
| estimatedLoad | yes | number | {"minimum":0,"maximum":100} |

Example:

```json
{
  "estimatedLoad": 1,
  "role": "example"
}
```

## AccountAvailability

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountAvailability.json), input validation.

Type: ["available","near_limit","exhausted","logged_out","unknown"]. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"exhausted"
```

## AccountDirectory

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountDirectory.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Absolute Unix or drive-qualified Windows directory.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"/example/home"
```

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountEnvKey.json), input validation.

Type: ["CODEX_HOME","CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR","XDG_DATA_HOME","XDG_CONFIG_HOME","XDG_STATE_HOME","XDG_CACHE_HOME","CURSOR_DATA_DIR"]. See JSON Schema for constraints.

```json
"CURSOR_DATA_DIR"
```

## AccountQuota

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountQuota.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| auth | yes | ["logged_in","logged_out","unknown"] |  |
| cursorSdkAuth | no | [CursorSdkAuth.json](schema/CursorSdkAuth.json) |  |
| observedAt | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| windows | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"type":"string","maxLength":128},"additionalProperties":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/QuotaWindow.json"},"maxProperties":32,"x-ace-constraint":"At most 32 quota windows."} |
| blockers | no | object | {"default":{},"properties":{"overflow":{"type":"boolean","const":true},"limitError":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/QuotaWindow.json"}}} |
| usage | yes | object | {"properties":{"inputTokens":{"type":"number","minimum":0},"outputTokens":{"type":"number","minimum":0},"costUsd":{"type":"number","minimum":0}}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "auth": "logged_in",
  "cursorSdkAuth": {
    "source": "none",
    "status": "logged-in"
  },
  "observedAt": 5,
  "usage": {
    "inputTokens": 8
  },
  "windows": {}
}
```

## Agent

| origin | yes | [AgentOrigin.json](schema/AgentOrigin.json) |  |
| lineage | no | [ThreadLineage.json](schema/ThreadLineage.json) |  |

| fidelity | yes | [AgentFidelity.json](schema/AgentFidelity.json) |  |
| spawnedBy | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ItemId.json"},{"type":"null"}]} |
| name | no | string |  |
| role | no | string |  |
| model | no | string |  |
| cwd | yes | string |  |
| status | yes | [AgentStatus.json](schema/AgentStatus.json) |  |

```json
  "background": false,

  "cwd": "example",
  "fidelity": "placeholder",

    "lossy": true,
    "mode": "native",
    "parentAgentId": "example",
    "parentThreadId": "example",
    "point": {
      "itemId": "example",
      "type": "item"
    }
  },
  "model": "example",
  "native": {
    "path": "example",
    "provider": "antigravity"
  },
  "origin": "ace",
  "parentId": null,
  "spawnedBy": null,
  "status": {
    "message": "example",
    "on": "human",
    "state": "blocked"
  },
  "threadId": "example"
}
```

| phase | yes | ["downloading","verifying","extracting","ready"] |  |
| received | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| total | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |

```json
}
```

[JSON Schema](schema/CommandsList.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Exactly one of threadId or draft is required.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| threadId | no | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |

| type | yes | `"commands.list"` |  |
| query | no | string | {"default":"","maxLength":256} |
| limit | no | integer | {"default":50,"minimum":1,"maximum":100} |

Example:

```json
{
  "draft": {
    "draftId": "example",
    "instanceId": "example",
    "provider": "antigravity",
    "workspaceId": "example"
  },
  "limit": 2,
  "query": "example",
  "requestId": "example",
  "type": "commands.list"
}
```

### events

Semantic rule: throughSeq >= afterSeq; events are ordered with (firstSeq ?? seq) > previous seq and seq <= throughSeq.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"events"` |  |
| subscriptionId | yes | string |  |
| afterSeq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |

| events | yes | array | {"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/DeliveryEvent.json"}} |

Example:

```json

  "afterSeq": 4,
  "events": [],
  "subscriptionId": "example",
  "throughSeq": 9,
  "type": "events"
}
```

| phase | yes | ["created","running","cancelling","settled"] |  |
| generation | no | integer | {"default":0,"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| outcome | no | [DelegationOutcome.json](schema/DelegationOutcome.json) |  |
| resolvedModel | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| resultDelivery | no | ["parent","owner"] |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "childId": "example",
  "createdAt": 7,
  "depth": 1,
  "parentAgentId": "example",
  "parentId": "example",
  "phase": "cancelling",
  "request": {
    "acpAgentId": "example",
    "installationId": "example",
    "options": {},
    "permissionMode": "read-only",
    "provider": "opencode",
    "requestId": "nyp",
    "role": "example",
    "task": "example"
  },
  "requestId": "fsz5mDoIyk",
  "rootId": "example"
}
```

## DelegationRequest

[JSON Schema](schema/DelegationRequest.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128,"pattern":"^[a-zA-Z0-9_.-]+$"} |
| task | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":16384} |
| role | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":1024} |
| provider | yes | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| permissionMode | no | [PermissionMode.json](schema/PermissionMode.json) |  |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| model | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| accountId | no | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| options | no | [AgentLaunchOptions.json](schema/AgentLaunchOptions.json) |  |
| wait | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| estimatedLoad | no | number | {"default":0,"minimum":0,"maximum":100} |

Example:

```json
{
  "model": "example",
  "provider": "pi",
  "requestId": "g.FmmmxtVqw",
  "role": "example",
  "task": "example"
}
```

## DeviceSettings

[JSON Schema](schema/DeviceSettings.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| appearance | no | ["light","dark"] |  |
| location | no | object | {"properties":{"latitude":{"type":"number","minimum":-90,"maximum":90},"longitude":{"type":"number","minimum":-180,"maximum":180}},"required":["latitude","longitude"]} |
| locale | no | string | {"pattern":"^[a-zA-Z]{2,8}(?:[-_][a-zA-Z0-9]{2,8}){0,2}$"} |

Example:

```json
{
  "appearance": "dark",
  "location": {
    "latitude": 9,
    "longitude": 9
  }
}
```

## ItemsWindowRequest

[JSON Schema](schema/ItemsWindowRequest.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Exactly one target; before + after + target is at most 200 items.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"items.window"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| aroundSeq | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| turnOrdinal | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| before | no | integer | {"default":50,"minimum":0,"maximum":199} |
| after | no | integer | {"default":50,"minimum":0,"maximum":199} |

Example:

```json
{
  "after": 5,
  "aroundSeq": 1,
  "before": 2,
  "requestId": "example",
  "threadId": "example",
  "type": "items.window"
}
```

[JSON Schema](schema/ItemsWindowResponse.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"items.window"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |

| targetSeq | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]} |
| items | yes | array | {"maxItems":200,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/Item.json"}} |
| itemSeqs | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"type":"string"},"additionalProperties":{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991}} |
| itemsBefore | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]} |
| itemsAfter | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "itemSeqs": {},
  "items": [],
  "itemsAfter": 9,
  "itemsBefore": null,
  "requestId": "example",

  "threadId": "example",
  "type": "items.window"
```
```json
}
```

## PortableHandoff

[JSON Schema](schema/PortableHandoff.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

| sourceThreadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| origin | no | object | {"properties":{"provider":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProviderKind.json"},"backend":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256}},"required":["provider"]} |
| throughSeq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| lossy | yes | `true` |  |
| policy | yes | `"recent-complete-items"` |  |
| excerpts | yes | array | {"maxItems":200,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"citation":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/HandoffCitation.json"},"text":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}},"required":["citation","text"]}} |
| omittedItems | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| history | yes | object | {"properties":{"type":{"type":"string","const":"items.page"},"tool":{"default":"ace_read_handoff","type":"string","const":"ace_read_handoff"},"chunkTool":{"default":"ace_read_handoff_chunk","type":"string","const":"ace_read_handoff_chunk"},"threadId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ThreadId.json"},"before":{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"limit":{"type":"number","const":50}},"required":["type","threadId","before","limit"]} |
| limitations | yes | `"Provider-private state is unavailable. Page history for omitted items, full text, reasoning, tools and attachments."` |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "excerpts": [],
  "history": {
    "before": 3,
    "limit": 50,
    "threadId": "example",
    "type": "items.page"
  },
  "limitations": "Provider-private state is unavailable. Page history for omitted items, full text, reasoning, tools and attachments.",
  "lossy": true,
  "omittedItems": 3,
  "policy": "recent-complete-items",
  "sourceThreadId": "example",
  "throughSeq": 2,
  "version": 1
}
```

## ProviderInstance

[JSON Schema](schema/ProviderInstance.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Native instances use native account IDs. ACP instances require matching agent, installation and instance references, id equal to instanceId, default_cli home strategy and an empty selector environment.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | yes | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| homeStrategy | no | `"default_cli"` |  |
| loginRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| profileRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| installationVersion | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| provider | yes | [AccountProvider.json](schema/AccountProvider.json) |  |
| label | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| homeDir | yes | [AccountDirectory.json](schema/AccountDirectory.json) |  |
| env | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AccountEnvKey.json"},"additionalProperties":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AccountDirectory.json"}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "env": {
    "CODEX_HOME": "/example/codex"
  },
  "homeDir": "/example/codex",
  "id": "codex-default",
  "label": "Codex",
  "provider": "codex"
}
```

| sessionId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| source | yes | [ReviewExecutionSource.json](schema/ReviewExecutionSource.json) |  |
| diff | yes | string | {"maxLength":65536} |

Example:

```json
{
  "diff": "example",
  "kind": "review-run",
  "requestId": "example",
  "sessionId": "example",
  "source": {
    "from": {
      "kind": "commit",
      "ref": "example"
    },
    "to": {
      "kind": "working-tree"
    },
    "workspaceId": "example",
    "worktree": "example"
  },
  "threadId": "example"
}
```

## ScreenCapabilities

[JSON Schema](schema/ScreenCapabilities.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| version | yes | `2` |  |
| platform | yes | ["macos","windows","linux-x11","linux-wayland"] |  |
| capture | yes | object | {"properties":{"windows":{"type":"boolean"},"displays":{"type":"boolean"},"changeDriven":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["windows","displays","changeDriven"]} |
| input | yes | object | {"properties":{"pointer":{"type":"boolean"},"keyboard":{"type":"boolean"},"scroll":{"type":"boolean"},"text":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["pointer","keyboard","scroll","text"]} |
| uiTree | yes | boolean |  |
| semanticActions | yes | array | {"maxItems":6,"items":{"type":"string","enum":["press","focus","setValue","scroll","expand","select"]}} |
| codecs | yes | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":2,"items":{"type":"string","enum":["jpeg","h264"]}} |
| permissions | yes | object | {"properties":{"screen":{"type":"string","enum":["granted","denied","prompt","n/a"]},"input":{"type":"string","enum":["granted","denied","prompt","n/a"]}},"required":["screen","input"]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "capture": {
    "changeDriven": true,
    "displays": false,
    "windows": true
  },
  "codecs": [
    "h264"
  ],
  "input": {
    "keyboard": false,
    "pointer": false,
    "scroll": false,
    "text": false
  },
  "permissions": {
    "input": "denied",
    "screen": "prompt"
  },
  "platform": "linux-wayland",
  "semanticActions": [],
  "uiTree": false,
  "version": 2
}
```

| kind | no | [SearchKind.json](schema/SearchKind.json) |  |

## UsageQuery

[JSON Schema](schema/UsageQuery.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: to >= from, their difference is <= 365 days, and groupBy entries are unique.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| from | yes | string | {"format":"date","pattern":"^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]\|\\d\\d[13579][26]\|\\d\\d0[48]\|[02468][048]00\|[13579][26]00)-02-29\|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]\|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|3[01])\|(?:0[469]\|11)-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|30)\|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]\|1\\d\|2[0-8])))$"} |
| to | yes | string | {"format":"date","pattern":"^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]\|\\d\\d[13579][26]\|\\d\\d0[48]\|[02468][048]00\|[13579][26]00)-02-29\|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]\|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|3[01])\|(?:0[469]\|11)-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|30)\|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]\|1\\d\|2[0-8])))$"} |
| groupBy | no | array | {"default":[],"maxItems":7,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/UsageDimension.json"}} |
| filters | no | object | {"default":{},"propertyNames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/UsageDimension.json"},"additionalProperties":{"minItems":1,"maxItems":50,"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":8192}}} |
| agentTree | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |
| limit | no | integer | {"default":100,"minimum":1,"maximum":1000} |
| orderBy | no | ["tokens","cost"] | {"default":"tokens"} |
| equivalentApiCost | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| quotaAccount | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |

Example:

```json
{
  "from": "2026-10-01",
  "to": "2026-10-02"
}
```

| unknownBillingTokens | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |

Example:

```json
{
  "cacheWrite1hTokens": 2,
  "cacheWriteTokens": 6,
  "cachedInputTokens": 5,
  "equivalentApiUsd": 6,
  "estimatedUsd": 1,
  "inputTokens": 1,
  "outputTokens": 0,
  "providerReportedUsd": 7,
  "reasoningTokens": 7,
  "subscriptionTokens": 0,
  "unknownBillingTokens": 1,
  "unpricedTokens": 0
}
```

## UsageUpdated

[JSON Schema](schema/UsageUpdated.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: If usageScope is provider_session or model_session, counterKey is required.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| usageScope | no | ["agent","provider_session","model_session"] |  |
| counterKey | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |
| contextTokens | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| contextSessionId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |
| reasoningTokens | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| cacheWriteTokens | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| cacheWrite1hTokens | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| model | no | string | {"minLength":1} |
| accountId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |
| billingMode | no | ["api","subscription","unknown"] |  |
| counterMode | no | ["cumulative","incremental"] |  |
| type | yes | `"usage.updated"` |  |
| agentId | yes | [AgentId.json](schema/AgentId.json) |  |
| inputTokens | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| outputTokens | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| cachedInputTokens | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| contextWindow | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| costUsd | no | number | {"minimum":0} |

Example:

```json
{
  "accountId": "example",
  "agentId": "example",
  "contextSessionId": "example",
  "contextWindow": 10,
  "counterKey": "example",
  "inputTokens": 2,
  "outputTokens": 8,
  "type": "usage.updated",
  "usageScope": "model_session"
}
```

[JSON Schema](schema/WebPushSubscription.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |

Whitespace, Unicode hosts, opaque schemes and parser-normalized URLs are allowed. x-ace-url-minLength and x-ace-url-maxLength apply after trimming whitespace and removing ASCII tab, CR and LF characters, using UTF-16 code units.
